    75	import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
    76	import { BoundaryRow } from "./BoundaryRow";
    77	import { TranscriptView, renderBodyWithCode } from "./TranscriptView";
    78	import { useParsedSession } from "./useParsedSession";
    79	import type { ParsedSession, ParseWarning, SkimBlock } from "./parsers";
    80	import type { SessionRow } from "./types";
    81	import "./SkimView.css";
    82	
    83	/**
    84	 * Stagger cap per spec line 75 + 1100 ("Skim-block stagger capped at
    85	 * 8 blocks"). Block index >= 8 all receive the 320 ms delay.
    86	 */
    87	const STAGGER_CAP = 8;
    88	/**
    89	 * Per-step delay multiplier per spec table row 9 ("40 ms × N").
    90	 */
    91	const STAGGER_STEP_MS = 40;
    92	
    93	export type SkimViewProps = {
    94	  /** Selected session row. */
    95	  row: SessionRow;
    96	  /**
    97	   * Pinned-`now` ISO string. Forwarded to the scoped TranscriptView
    98	   * mounted under user_turn / agent_only disclosures so timestamps
    99	   * render deterministically in tests.
   100	   */
   101	  now: string;
   102	};
   103	
   104	export function SkimView({ row, now }: SkimViewProps) {
   105	  const result = useParsedSession(row);
   106	  const [warningsBannerDismissed, setWarningsBannerDismissed] =
   107	    useState<boolean>(false);
   108	
   109	  // Defensive reset: `<SessionView key={selectedRowKey}>` already
   110	  // destroys this component on selection change, but the belt-and-
   111	  // suspenders `useEffect` clears the dismissed-state if `row.rowKey`
   112	  // changes without a parent-level remount (mirrors TranscriptView).
   113	  useEffect(() => {
   114	    setWarningsBannerDismissed(false);
   115	  }, [row.rowKey]);
   116	
   117	  if (result.state === "idle") {
   118	    return (
   119	      <p className="skim-empty">Select a session to read its skim outline.</p>
   120	    );
   121	  }
   122	  if (result.state === "no_raw") {
   123	    return (
   124	      <p className="skim-not-imported">
   125	        This session has not been imported yet — only the source-side
   126	        metadata is available. Click <strong>Import</strong> in the
   127	        action bar to fetch the raw payload.
   128	      </p>
   129	    );
   130	  }
   131	  if (result.state === "loading") {
   132	    return <p className="skim-loading">Reading session…</p>;
   133	  }
   134	  if (result.state === "error") {
   135	    return (
   136	      <div className="skim-error-block">
   137	        <p className="skim-error">
   138	          Could not load session: {result.error.message}.
   139	        </p>
   140	        <p>
   141	          <button
   142	            type="button"
   143	            className="skim-retry"
   144	            onClick={result.retry}
   145	          >
   146	            Retry
   147	          </button>
   148	        </p>
   149	      </div>
   150	    );
   151	  }
   152	  // success | truncated
   153	  return (
   154	    <SkimBody
   155	      parsed={result.parsed}
   156	      now={now}
   157	      truncated={result.state === "truncated"}
   158	      row={row}
   159	      warningsBannerDismissed={warningsBannerDismissed}
   160	      onDismissWarnings={() => setWarningsBannerDismissed(true)}
   161	    />
   162	  );
   163	}
   164	
   165	function SkimBody({
   166	  parsed,
   167	  now,
   168	  truncated,
   169	  row,
   170	  warningsBannerDismissed,
   171	  onDismissWarnings,
   172	}: {
   173	  parsed: ParsedSession;
   174	  now: string;
   175	  truncated: boolean;
   176	  row: SessionRow;
   177	  warningsBannerDismissed: boolean;
   178	  onDismissWarnings: () => void;
   179	}) {
   180	  return (
   181	    <section className="skim-body" aria-label="Session skim outline">
   182	      {truncated ? <TruncationBanner /> : null}
   183	      {parsed.warnings.length > 0 && !warningsBannerDismissed ? (
   184	        <ParseWarningsBanner
   185	          warnings={parsed.warnings}
   186	          onDismiss={onDismissWarnings}
   187	        />
   188	      ) : null}
   189	      {parsed.skim.length === 0 ? (
   190	        <p className="skim-empty-stream">No skim blocks parsed.</p>
   191	      ) : (
   192	        <ol className="skim-stream">
   193	          {parsed.skim.map((block, idx) => (
   194	            <SkimBlockRow
   195	              key={`${block.kind}-${block.start}-${block.end}`}
   196	              block={block}
   197	              parsed={parsed}
   198	              now={now}
   199	              row={row}
   200	              staggerIndex={Math.min(idx, STAGGER_CAP)}
   201	            />
   202	          ))}
   203	        </ol>
   204	      )}
   205	    </section>
   206	  );
   207	}

   272	function MessageRow({ msg, now }: { msg: Message; now: string }) {
   273	  switch (msg.kind) {
   274	    case "user":
   275	      return <UserMessage msg={msg} now={now} />;
   276	    case "assistant":
   277	      return <AssistantMessage msg={msg} now={now} />;
   278	    case "tool_use":
   279	      return <ToolUseMessage msg={msg} now={now} />;
   280	    case "tool_result":
   281	      return <ToolResultMessage msg={msg} now={now} />;
   282	    case "system":
   283	      return <SystemMessage msg={msg} />;
   284	    case "boundary":
   285	      return <BoundaryMessage msg={msg} />;
   286	    case "unknown":
   287	      return <UnknownMessage msg={msg} />;
   288	    default: {
   289	      // Exhaustiveness: a future MessageKind without a render branch
   290	      // breaks the build here. Per m4-plan §6 catch #13.
   291	      const _exhaustive: never = msg.kind;
   292	      void _exhaustive;
   293	      return null;
   294	    }
   295	  }
   296	}

   353	function ToolResultMessage({ msg, now }: { msg: Message; now: string }) {
   354	  const toolName = msg.toolName ?? "(unknown)";
   355	  const overflow = msg.bytes > TOOL_RESULT_OVERFLOW_BYTES;
   356	  const { head, tail, tailBytes } = overflow
   357	    ? splitToolResult(msg.text, msg.bytes)
   358	    : { head: msg.text, tail: null, tailBytes: 0 };

   367	          <MessageTime iso={msg.timestamp} now={now} />
   368	        </header>
   369	        <pre className="msg-tool-pre msg-tool-result-head">{head}</pre>
   370	        {overflow && tail !== null ? (
   371	          <details className="msg-tool-disclosure msg-tool-overflow">
   372	            <summary>Expand ({tailBytes} more bytes)</summary>
   373	            <pre className="msg-tool-pre msg-tool-result-tail">{tail}</pre>
   374	          </details>

   414	// ============================================================================
   415	// Helpers
   416	// ============================================================================
   417	
   418	/**
   419	 * Slice an unknown line per spec line 711 + design.md §3.7. The
   420	 * trailing U+2026 ellipsis is appended ONLY when the original text
   421	 * exceeded the 80-char prefix; otherwise the original text is
   422	 * returned verbatim.
   423	 *
   424	 * @internal — exported for testing.
   425	 */
   426	export function renderUnknownLine(text: string): string {
   427	  if (text.length > UNKNOWN_LINE_PREFIX_CHARS) {
   428	    return text.slice(0, UNKNOWN_LINE_PREFIX_CHARS) + "…";
   429	  }
   430	  return text;
   431	}
   432	
   433	/**
   434	 * Render a message body with naive code-fence detection per
   435	 * design.md §3.1 + Q-DESIGN-4. Triple-backtick fenced blocks become
   436	 * `<pre class="msg-code-block">`; single-backtick spans become
   437	 * `<code class="msg-code-inline">`. Unterminated fences and
   438	 * cross-line single-backtick spans render as plain text (per
   439	 * design.md §13 Q-DESIGN-4).
   440	 *
   441	 * The detector is render-time (Q1 in m4-plan §5) so the parser
   442	 * surface stays stable.
   443	 */
   444	export function renderBodyWithCode(text: string): ReactNode[] {
   445	  const out: ReactNode[] = [];
   446	  // Fence-pair scan first. Triple-backtick is the strongest signal;
   447	  // resolve it before single-backtick inline code.
   448	  const FENCE = "```";
   449	  let cursor = 0;
   450	  let key = 0;
   451	  while (cursor < text.length) {
   452	    const fenceStart = text.indexOf(FENCE, cursor);
   453	    if (fenceStart === -1) {
   454	      // No more fences — emit the remainder, possibly with inline code.
   455	      out.push(...renderInlineSegment(text.slice(cursor), key));
   456	      break;
   457	    }
   458	    // Emit text before the fence (with inline-code detection).
   459	    if (fenceStart > cursor) {
   460	      out.push(...renderInlineSegment(text.slice(cursor, fenceStart), key));
   461	      key = out.length;
   462	    }
   463	    // Find the matching closing fence.
   464	    const innerStart = fenceStart + FENCE.length;
   465	    const fenceEnd = text.indexOf(FENCE, innerStart);
   466	    if (fenceEnd === -1) {
   467	      // Unterminated fence: render the rest as plain text per
   468	      // design.md §13 Q-DESIGN-4 case 2.
   469	      out.push(...renderInlineSegment(text.slice(fenceStart), key));
   470	      break;
   471	    }
   472	    // Strip the optional language hint after the opening fence.
   473	    let blockBody = text.slice(innerStart, fenceEnd);
   474	    const firstNewline = blockBody.indexOf("\n");
   475	    if (firstNewline !== -1) {
   476	      // Drop everything before the first newline (the language hint).
   477	      blockBody = blockBody.slice(firstNewline + 1);
   478	    }
   479	    out.push(
   480	      <pre
   481	        key={`code-block-${key}`}
   482	        className="msg-code-block"
   483	      >
   484	        {blockBody}
   485	      </pre>,
   486	    );
   487	    key = out.length;
   488	    cursor = fenceEnd + FENCE.length;
   489	  }
   490	  return out;
   491	}
   492	
   493	/**
   494	 * Inline-code detection within a non-fenced text segment. Splits on
   495	 * single-backtick pairs and emits `<code class="msg-code-inline">`
   496	 * for each match. Pairs spanning a newline are NOT treated as
   497	 * inline code (per design.md §13 Q-DESIGN-4 case 6).
   498	 */
   499	function renderInlineSegment(text: string, baseKey: number): ReactNode[] {
   500	  if (text.length === 0) return [];
   501	  const parts: ReactNode[] = [];
   502	  let cursor = 0;
   503	  let key = baseKey;
   504	  while (cursor < text.length) {
   505	    const open = text.indexOf("`", cursor);
   506	    if (open === -1) {
   507	      parts.push(text.slice(cursor));
   508	      break;
   509	    }
   510	    if (open > cursor) {
   511	      parts.push(text.slice(cursor, open));
   512	    }
   513	    const close = text.indexOf("`", open + 1);
   514	    if (close === -1) {
   515	      // Unmatched single backtick → emit as plain text.
   516	      parts.push(text.slice(open));
   517	      break;
   518	    }
   519	    const inner = text.slice(open + 1, close);
   520	    if (inner.includes("\n")) {
   521	      // Cross-line backtick pair → render as plain text.
   522	      parts.push(text.slice(open, close + 1));
   523	    } else {
   524	      parts.push(
   525	        <code key={`code-inline-${key}`} className="msg-code-inline">
   526	          {inner}
   527	        </code>,
   528	      );
   529	      key += 1;
   530	    }
   531	    cursor = close + 1;
   532	  }
   533	  return parts;
   534	}
   535	
   536	/**
   537	 * Split a tool_result body at the 2 KB byte boundary, walking back
   538	 * to a UTF-8 codepoint boundary so we never split mid-codepoint.
   539	 * Per design.md §9.2 + Q2 in m4-plan §5.
   540	 *
   541	 * @internal — exported for testing.
   542	 */
   543	export function splitToolResult(
   544	  text: string,
   545	  totalBytes: number,
   546	): { head: string; tail: string; tailBytes: number } {
   547	  const enc = new TextEncoder();
   548	  const dec = new TextDecoder();
   549	  const all = enc.encode(text);
   550	  // Defensive: parser-computed `msg.bytes` is approximate. Recompute
   551	  // from the actual bytes so the split arithmetic is always
   552	  // consistent with `all`.
   553	  const trueBytes = all.length;
   554	  if (trueBytes <= TOOL_RESULT_OVERFLOW_BYTES) {
   555	    return { head: text, tail: "", tailBytes: 0 };
   556	  }
   557	  let cut = TOOL_RESULT_OVERFLOW_BYTES;
   558	  // UTF-8 continuation bytes start with bits 10xxxxxx (0x80..0xBF).
   559	  // Walk back until we land on a codepoint-leading byte.
   560	  while (cut > 0 && (all[cut] & 0xc0) === 0x80) {

exec

     1	// Transcript tab body for the Phase-5 right-pane Tabs shell.
     2	//
     3	// Phase 5 / Milestone 4 — first visible UI surface that consumes the
     4	// M3 parser pipeline (`useParsedSession` from M3b → typed messages
     5	// from M3a). Renders the chronological message list with per-kind
     6	// shells (user / assistant / tool_use / tool_result / system /
     7	// boundary / unknown), the truncation banner, the parse-warnings
     8	// dismissible disclosure, and the long-tool_result collapse. All
     9	// copy is verbatim from the spec at `working/phase-5.md` lines
    10	// 699–717 (frozen at 05467ad).
    11	//
    12	// Architecture (per design.md §2):
    13	//   - Top-level state-discriminator switches on
    14	//     `useParsedSession(row).state`. `idle | no_raw | loading | error`
    15	//     each render a single muted line; `success | truncated` mount
    16	//     the `<TranscriptBody>` subtree.
    17	//   - `<TranscriptBody>` mounts the truncation banner (only when
    18	//     `state === "truncated"`), the parse-warnings disclosure (only
    19	//     when warnings.length > 0 AND not yet dismissed), and the
    20	//     per-message stream as an `<ol>`.
    21	//   - Each `<MessageRow>` switches on `msg.kind` and emits the kind-
    22	//     specific shell; the switch's default branch is the TypeScript
    23	//     exhaustiveness check (`const _: never = msg.kind`) so a future
    24	//     `MessageKind` addition fails the build until it gets a render
    25	//     branch.
    26	//
    27	// Critical correctness items (one entry per documented codex catch
    28	// precedent — see m4-plan §6):
    29	//   - **Spec-literal copy**: every visible string anchored to a spec
    30	//     line. Truncation banner per spec line 715. Parse-warnings per
    31	//     spec line 717 (including the grammatically-odd "1 parse
    32	//     warnings" for N=1 — design.md §7.1). Boundary per spec line
    33	//     710 ("SESSION RESUMED" / "CONVERSATION COMPACTED"). Unknown
    34	//     per spec line 711.
    35	//   - **Keep-mounted contract**: NO `key=` on the TranscriptView
    36	//     root or descendants except `<MessageRow key={msg.messageIndex}>`
    37	//     (content-keyed, NOT tab-keyed). Component-local state
    38	//     (warnings dismissal, expanded `<details>`) survives tab
    39	//     switches. The only natural reset is row identity change —
    40	//     handled by the parent `<SessionView key={selectedRowKey}>`
    41	//     remount + a defensive `useEffect` reset on `row.rowKey`.
    42	//   - **Token discipline**: zero new tokens. Every `var(--…)`
    43	//     reference resolves to a token in `apps/frontend/src/styles/
    44	//     tokens.css`. Hex isolation invariant preserved (24).
    45	//   - **Motion budget**: only `opacity` (truncation banner entrance)
    46	//     and `<details>` `block-size` (the M2b exemption). NO animated
    47	//     `color`, `border-color`, `width`, `height`, `top`, `padding`,
    48	//     `transform on message panels`, `background-color on message
    49	//     panels`. See TranscriptView.css for the explicit
    50	//     authorizations.
    51	//   - **a11y**: `<section aria-label="Session transcript">` anchors
    52	//     the surface; boundary `<li>` carries `role="separator"`
    53	//     directly (no nested `<div>` per design.md §12.2);
    54	//     `<details>` summaries are keyboard-focusable; Retry button is
    55	//     focusable + accessible-name "Retry".
    56	//
    57	// @see working/phase-5.md:699-717 (Transcript tab body)
    58	// @see working/phase-5/designs/m4-transcript/design.md (full design)
    59	// @see working/phase-5/m4-plan.md (implementation plan)
    60

    62	import { BoundaryRow } from "./BoundaryRow";
    63	import { relativeTimeFrom } from "./relativeTime";

    69	/**
    70	 * Threshold for the `tool_result` body collapse. Per spec line 708
    71	 * + Q2 in m4-plan §5: the discriminator is the parser-computed
    72	 * `msg.bytes` (UTF-8 byte length), NOT `msg.text.length` (which
    73	 * undercounts multi-byte codepoints).

    75	 * Declared as a private constant rather than a token because the
    76	 * 2 KB threshold is structural rendering logic, not a design-token
    77	 * concern. Documented here so codex review can verify the value.
    78	 */
    79	const TOOL_RESULT_OVERFLOW_BYTES = 2048;
    80	
    81	/**
    82	 * Maximum prefix length for the `unknown` fallback line. Per spec
    83	 * line 711: "Unrecognized line: {first 80 chars}…".

    85	const UNKNOWN_LINE_PREFIX_CHARS = 80;
    86	
    87	export type TranscriptViewProps = {
    88	  /** Selected session row. */
    89	  row: SessionRow;
    90	  /**
    91	   * Pinned-`now` ISO string used for relative-time labelling. Required
    92	   * so timestamps render deterministically in tests (mirrors
    93	   * SessionMetadata's contract).
    94	   */
    95	  now: string;
    96	  /**
    97	   * Optional inclusive [start, end] messageIndex range. When provided,
    98	   * the body renders only `parsed.messages.slice(start, end + 1)`.
    99	   * Used by SkimView (M5) to mount a scoped TranscriptView inside the
   100	   * "Expand to raw messages" affordance and the agent_only block
   101	   * expansion (Resolved Decision #9). Out-of-bounds values clamp to
   102	   * `[0, parsed.messages.length - 1]`. When `start > end` (e.g., the
   103	   * empty-stream sentinel `{start: 0, end: -1}`) the body slice is
   104	   * empty and the empty-stream copy renders.
   105	   *
   106	   * Omitting this prop renders the full transcript (M4 default
   107	   * behaviour — backward compatible).
   108	   */
   109	  messageRange?: { start: number; end: number };
   110	};
   111	
   112	export function TranscriptView({ row, now, messageRange }: TranscriptViewProps) {
   113	  const result = useParsedSession(row);
   114	  const [warningsBannerDismissed, setWarningsBannerDismissed] =
   115	    useState<boolean>(false);
   116	
   117	  // Defensive reset: `<SessionView key={selectedRowKey}>` already
   118	  // destroys this component on selection change, but the belt-and-
   119	  // suspenders `useEffect` clears the dismissed-state if `row.rowKey`
   120	  // changes without a parent-level remount. Mirrors SessionView's
   121	  // own pattern at lines 223-226.
   122	  useEffect(() => {
   123	    setWarningsBannerDismissed(false);
   124	  }, [row.rowKey]);
   125	
   126	  if (result.state === "idle") {
   127	    return (
   128	      <p className="transcript-empty">Select a session to read its transcript.</p>
   129	    );
   130	  }
   131	  if (result.state === "no_raw") {
   132	    return (
   133	      <p className="transcript-not-imported">
   134	        This session has not been imported yet — only the source-side
   135	        metadata is available. Click <strong>Import</strong> in the
   136	        action bar to fetch the raw payload.
   137	      </p>
   138	    );
   139	  }
   140	  if (result.state === "loading") {
   141	    return <p className="transcript-loading">Reading session…</p>;
   142	  }
   143	  if (result.state === "error") {
   144	    return (
   145	      <div className="transcript-error-block">
   146	        <p className="transcript-error">
   147	          Could not load session: {result.error.message}.
   148	        </p>
   149	        <p>
   150	          <button
   151	            type="button"
   152	            className="transcript-retry"
   153	            onClick={result.retry}
   154	          >
   155	            Retry
   156	          </button>
   157	        </p>
   158	      </div>
   159	    );
   160	  }
   161	  // success | truncated
   162	  return (
   163	    <TranscriptBody
   164	      parsed={result.parsed}
   165	      now={now}
   166	      truncated={result.state === "truncated"}
   167	      warningsBannerDismissed={warningsBannerDismissed}
   168	      onDismissWarnings={() => setWarningsBannerDismissed(true)}
   169	      messageRange={messageRange}
   170	    />
   171	  );
   172	}
   173	
   174	function TranscriptBody({
   175	  parsed,
   176	  now,
   177	  truncated,
   178	  warningsBannerDismissed,
   179	  onDismissWarnings,
   180	  messageRange,
   181	}: {
   182	  parsed: ParsedSession;
   183	  now: string;
   184	  truncated: boolean;
   185	  warningsBannerDismissed: boolean;
   186	  onDismissWarnings: () => void;
   187	  messageRange?: { start: number; end: number };
   188	}) {
   189	  // Defensive slice with clamping. Per design.md §10.3 + planner Q10:
   190	  //   - missing prop -> render the full message stream (M4 default).
   191	  //   - start < 0       -> clamp to 0.
   192	  //   - end >= len      -> clamp to len - 1.
   193	  //   - start > end     -> empty slice (renders the empty-stream copy).
   194	  //     This includes the buildSkim empty-stream sentinel
   195	  //     `{start: 0, end: -1}` per spec line 697.
   196	  const slicedMessages = (() => {
   197	    if (!messageRange) return parsed.messages;
   198	    const lo = Math.max(0, messageRange.start);
   199	    const hi = Math.min(parsed.messages.length - 1, messageRange.end);
   200	    if (hi < lo) return [];
   201	    return parsed.messages.slice(lo, hi + 1);
   202	  })();
   203	  return (
   204	    <section className="transcript-body" aria-label="Session transcript">
   205	      {truncated ? <TruncationBanner /> : null}
   206	      {parsed.warnings.length > 0 && !warningsBannerDismissed ? (
   207	        <ParseWarningsBanner
   208	          warnings={parsed.warnings}
   209	          onDismiss={onDismissWarnings}
   210	        />
   211	      ) : null}
   212	      {slicedMessages.length === 0 ? (
   213	        <p className="transcript-empty-stream">No messages parsed.</p>
   214	      ) : (
   215	        <ol className="transcript-stream">
   216	          {slicedMessages.map((msg) => (
   217	            <MessageRow key={msg.messageIndex} msg={msg} now={now} />
   218	          ))}
   219	        </ol>
   220	      )}
   221	    </section>
   222	  );
   223	}
   224	
   225	function TruncationBanner() {
   226	  return (
   227	    <div
   228	      className="transcript-banner transcript-banner-truncation"
   229	      role="status"
   230	    >
   231	      Truncated at 5 MB — full payload not parsed. Use the{" "}
   232	      <strong>Open raw</strong> anchor in the session header to
   233	      inspect the full payload.
   234	    </div>
   235	  );
   236	}
   237	
   238	function ParseWarningsBanner({
   239	  warnings,
   240	  onDismiss,
   241	}: {
   242	  warnings: ParseWarning[];
   243	  onDismiss: () => void;
   244	}) {
   245	  // JSDoc rationale (do NOT "fix" the grammar):
   246	  // The spec at working/phase-5.md:717 is treated as a literal
   247	  // string. For N=1 this reads as "1 parse warnings — click to
   248	  // view." which is grammatically odd in English but spec-precedent
   249	  // compliant. Codex verified spec literals beat English grammar
   250	  // in M3a r1; do NOT swap in a singular form.
   251	  return (
   252	    <details className="transcript-banner transcript-banner-warnings">
   253	      <summary>{warnings.length} parse warnings — click to view.</summary>
   254	      <ul className="transcript-warnings-list">
   255	        {warnings.map((w, idx) => (
   256	          <li key={`${w.lineOrdinal}-${idx}`}>
   257	            line {w.lineOrdinal} · {w.reason}
   258	          </li>
   259	        ))}
   260	      </ul>

   254	function SkimBlockRow({
   255	  block,
   256	  parsed,
   257	  now,
   258	  row,

   307	      void _exhaustive;
   308	      return null;
   309	    }
   310	  }

   373	function AgentOnlyBlock({
   374	  block,
   375	  row,
   376	  now,
   377	  staggerIndex,
   378	}: {
   379	  block: SkimBlock;
   380	  row: SessionRow;
   381	  now: string;
   382	  staggerIndex: number;
   383	}) {
   384	  // Inclusive count: end - start + 1. Empty-stream sentinel

   408	function OversizedUserMessageBlock({
   409	  block,
   410	  parsed,
   411	  staggerIndex,
   412	}: {

   435	        </div>

   439	}

     1	// Right-pane shell for the Phase-5 split-pane master-detail layout.
     2	//
     3	// M2b rewires from the M1a four-state placeholder to a four-tab shell.
     4	// The state machine is preserved verbatim from M1a, with one
     5	// substitution: the M1a `ready-placeholder` state is REPLACED by
     6	// `ready` (the tab shell mounts inside it). Empty / loading /
     7	// session_not_found are unchanged from M1a.
     8	//
     9	// Tab shell (per design.md §3.2):
    10	//   - Minimal header: title (Fraunces italic at --text-xl) + tool
    11	//     badge + status pill + optional conflict badge. Copy path,
    12	//     subagent badge, sourcePathIsStale hint, and "Open raw" header
    13	//     anchor all defer to M4.
    14	//   - Tabs primitive (Transcript / Skim / Raw / Metadata) below.
    15	//     Default tab AT M2b = "metadata" (Resolved Decision #11; M4
    16	//     shifts to "transcript").
    17	//   - Lazy panel mounting (Resolved Decision #12 + spec lines
    18	//     650–658): a panel renders ONLY if `tabId === activeTab` OR
    19	//     `visitedTabs.has(tabId)`. Once activated, the panel stays
    20	//     React-mounted for the rest of the selection — preserves
    21	//     RawTab's in-flight stream + future TranscriptView /
    22	//     SkimView state.
    23	//   - Cross-fade-IN-only animation: the panel's own
    24	//     `<div role="tabpanel">` toggles its inline `style.animation`
    25	//     between "tab-fade-in 120ms var(--ease-out) both" (active) and
    26	//     "none" (inactive). The browser fires the keyframe each time
    27	//     the property string transitions from "none" to a real
    28	//     animation. The panel's React subtree is STABLE — never
    29	//     remounted on tab change.
    30	//   - Page-turn fade: the outer `<article class="session-pane">`
    31	//     carries `key={selectedRowKey}` so every selection change is a
    32	//     fresh React mount; the `@keyframes session-page-turn` rule in
    33	//     SessionView.css fires automatically on mount.
    34	//
    35	// tabIndex matrix (design.md §6.1 + acceptance item 29a — Option A):
    36	//   - Skim, Transcript, AND Raw active panels carry `tabIndex={0}`
    37	//     (none of them have an unconditional focusable child, so the
    38	//     panel itself must be the first Tab stop).
    39	//   - Metadata does NOT carry `tabIndex` — its Copy path button is
    40	//     always rendered and always focusable.
    41	//   - Inactive panels (`hidden=true`) MUST NOT carry `tabIndex`.
    42	//

   162	function SessionNotFoundCopy({

   172	      <p className="not-found-hint">

   195	
   196	/**
   197	 * The four-tab shell that renders inside `state === "ready"`.
   198	 *
   199	 * Owns:
   200	 *   - `activeTab` state (controlled value passed to <Tabs>).
   201	 *   - `visitedTabs` Set (lazy-mount discipline).
   202	 *   - The minimal header (title / tool badge / status pill / optional
   203	 *     conflict badge).
   204	 *   - The four `<TabPanel>` wrappers with the cross-fade-IN-only
   205	 *     animation toggle.
   206	 *
   207	 * Reset on selection change is automatic — the parent
   208	 * `<SessionView key={selectedRowKey}>` (set in App.tsx) remounts
   209	 * this whole subtree, so `useState` re-initializes activeTab to
   210	 * `DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION` and visitedTabs to
   211	 * `new Set([DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION])`.
   212	 */
   213	function ReadyTabShell({
   214	  row,
   215	  now,
   216	}: {
   217	  row: SessionRow;
   218	  now: string;
   219	}) {
   220	  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabId>(DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION);
   221	  const [visitedTabs, setVisitedTabs] = useState<Set<TabId>>(
   222	    () => new Set<TabId>([DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION]),
   223	  );
   224	
   225	  // Defensive reset: if `row.rowKey` changes WITHOUT a parent-level
   226	  // remount (shouldn't happen given the App.tsx `key={selectedRowKey}`
   227	  // contract, but kept as a safety belt), snap activeTab back to the
   228	  // default and reset visitedTabs.
   229	  useEffect(() => {
   230	    setActiveTab(DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION);
   231	    setVisitedTabs(new Set<TabId>([DEFAULT_TAB_ON_SELECTION]));
   232	  }, [row.rowKey]);
   233	
   234	  const handleActivate = (next: TabId) => {
   235	    setActiveTab(next);
   236	    setVisitedTabs((prev) => {
   237	      if (prev.has(next)) return prev;
   238	      const nextSet = new Set(prev);
   239	      nextSet.add(next);
   240	      return nextSet;
   241	    });
   242	  };
   243	
   244	  // Object-keyed panel content — NEVER index-keyed (so an unvisited
   245	  // mid-sequence panel never causes React to re-key remaining panels
   246	  // by position). The OUTER `key={id}` on `<TabPanel>` is required;
   247	  // the INNER content tree must NOT carry any per-activation key
   248	  // (acceptance #27).
   249	  const panelContent: Record<TabId, ReactNode> = {
   250	    transcript: <TranscriptView row={row} now={now} />,
   251	    skim: <SkimView row={row} now={now} />,
   252	    raw: <RawTab row={row} />,
   253	    metadata: <SessionMetadata row={row} now={now} />,
   254	  };
   255	
   256	  const tabs = [
   257	    { id: "transcript" as const, label: "Transcript", panel: null },
   258	    { id: "skim" as const, label: "Skim", panel: null },
   259	    { id: "raw" as const, label: "Raw", panel: null },
   260	    { id: "metadata" as const, label: "Metadata", panel: null },
   261	  ];
   262	
   263	  return (
   264	    <>
   265	      <SessionPaneHeader row={row} />
   266	      <Tabs<TabId>
   267	        ariaLabel="Session content tabs"
   268	        value={activeTab}
   269	        onValueChange={handleActivate}
   270	        tabs={tabs}
   271	      />
   272	      <div className="session-pane-tabs">
   273	        {(Object.entries(panelContent) as Array<[TabId, ReactNode]>)
   274	          .filter(([id]) => visitedTabs.has(id))
   275	          .map(([id, content]) => (
   276	            <TabPanel key={id} id={id} isActive={id === activeTab}>
   277	              {content}
   278	            </TabPanel>
   279	          ))}
   280	      </div>
   281	    </>
   282	  );
   283	}
   284	
   285	function SessionPaneHeader({ row }: { row: SessionRow }) {
   286	  // M4 expansion (design.md §6.1.1): the "Open raw" anchor lands in
   287	  // the right-side action group AFTER the conflict badge, only when
   288	  // a stored UID exists (source-only rows would 404 on the raw URL).
   289	  // The anchor copy is "Open raw" verbatim per spec line 626 (NOT
   290	  // "View raw", which is the Metadata-tab precedent).
   291	  const rawHref =
   292	    row.storedSessionUid !== null
   293	      ? RAW_SESSION_PATH(row.storedSessionUid)
   294	      : null;
   295	  return (
   296	    <header className="session-pane-header">
   297	      <h2 className="session-title">{row.title ?? "(untitled)"}</h2>
   298	      <span className="session-tool-badge">{row.tool}</span>
   299	      <span className={`badge ${row.status.replace(/_/g, "-")}`}>
   300	        {row.status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
   301	      </span>
   302	      {row.statusConflict ? (
   303	        <span
   304	          className="badge session-conflict-badge"
   305	          title="Source and stored status disagreed during load — refresh to re-fetch."
   306	        >
   307	          Conflict
   308	        </span>
   309	      ) : null}
   310	      {rawHref !== null ? (
   311	        <a
   312	          className="session-open-raw"
   313	          href={rawHref}
   314	          target="_blank"
   315	          rel="noreferrer"
   316	        >
   317	          Open raw
   318	        </a>
   319	      ) : null}
   320	    </header>
   321	  );
   322	}
   323	
   324	/**
   325	 * One tab panel. Renders its `<div role="tabpanel">` always; the
   326	 * `hidden` HTML attribute toggles visibility. The panel's React
   327	 * subtree is STABLE — never remounted on tab change (Resolved
   328	 * Decision #12). The cross-fade-IN-only animation is driven by the
   329	 * inline `style.animation` toggle.
   330	 *
   331	 * tabIndex matrix (design.md §6.1 acceptance item 29a):
   332	 *   - Skim, Transcript, Raw → tabIndex=0 when active.
   333	 *   - Metadata              → no tabIndex (Copy path button is always
   334	 *                             focusable).
   335	 *   - Inactive (hidden)     → no tabIndex.
   336	 */
   337	function TabPanel({
   338	  id,
   339	  isActive,
   340	  children,
   341	}: {
   342	  id: TabId;
   343	  isActive: boolean;
   344	  children: ReactNode;
   345	}) {
   346	  const wantsTabIndex =
   347	    isActive && (id === "skim" || id === "transcript" || id === "raw");
   348	  return (
   349	    <div
   350	      role="tabpanel"
   351	      id={`panel-${id}`}
   352	      aria-labelledby={`tab-${id}`}
   353	      hidden={!isActive}
   354	      tabIndex={wantsTabIndex ? 0 : undefined}
   355	      style={{
   356	        animation: isActive
   357	          ? "tab-fade-in 120ms var(--ease-out) both"
   358	          : "none",
   359	      }}
   360	    >

 succeeded in 0ms:
   360	    >
   361	      {children}
   362	    </div>
   363	  );
   364	}

     1	/*
     2	 * BoundaryRow — Phase 5 / M5 shared chapter-break recipe.
     3	 *
     4	 * Extracted from TranscriptView.css (M4). Both `.msg-boundary*` and
     5	 * `.boundary-row*` selectors are declared here so existing M4 rhythm
     6	 * rules in TranscriptView.css (`.transcript-stream > .msg-boundary`)
     7	 * AND new SkimView rhythm rules (`.skim-stream > .boundary-row`)
     8	 * resolve to the same visual recipe.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""WCAG 2.1 contrast ratio computer for M5 SkimView surfaces.

Usage:
    python3 wcag_m5.py
    bun --bun python wcag_m5.py

Pure-stdlib (only `math`); no third-party deps. Reads no files; emits a
table for every pair declared in colors.md.

Pipeline (matches CSS Color L4 sec 10 + WCAG 2.1 sec 1.4.3):
    1. oklch -> oklab
    2. oklab -> linear sRGB (via Bjorn Ottosson's M2 matrix)
    3. linear sRGB -> nonlinear sRGB (piecewise gamma per IEC 61966-2-1)
    4. color-mix(in srgb, A p%, B q%): mix in nonlinear sRGB space, then
       reverse step 3 to get back to linear sRGB.
    5. Relative luminance: linear-sRGB R*0.2126 + G*0.7152 + B*0.0722
    6. Contrast: (L1 + 0.05) / (L2 + 0.05) where L1 >= L2.

This matches Chromium's `color-mix(in srgb)` implementation. The script
is byte-equivalent in math + matrices to wcag_m4.py; only the PAIRS list
differs. Rationale: M5 introduces ZERO new tokens, so the token table
must match M4 exactly. Drift between M4 and M5 ratios on shared pairs
(e.g. ink-on-surface) would indicate a math regression, not a design
change. Codex round 1 should re-run this script and diff its output
against M4's for shared pairs.
"""

import math
from typing import Tuple

# ----- M2a-canonical token oklch values (byte-equivalent to wcag_m4.py) -----
TOKENS = {
    "light": {
        "surface":        (0.98, 0.01, 70),
        "surface-raised": (0.96, 0.01, 70),
        "ink":            (0.20, 0.02, 70),
        "ink-muted":      (0.45, 0.02, 70),
        "border":         (0.85, 0.01, 70),
        "border-strong":  (0.65, 0.02, 70),
        "accent":         (0.55, 0.15, 50),
        "warn":           (0.58, 0.15, 60),
        "error":          (0.52, 0.18, 25),
    },
    "dark": {
        "surface":        (0.15, 0.01, 70),
        "surface-raised": (0.18, 0.01, 70),
        "ink":            (0.92, 0.01, 70),
        "ink-muted":      (0.70, 0.01, 70),
        "border":         (0.28, 0.01, 70),
        "border-strong":  (0.48, 0.02, 70),
        "accent":         (0.65, 0.15, 50),
        "warn":           (0.72, 0.13, 60),
        "error":          (0.68, 0.16, 25),
    },
}


def oklch_to_oklab(L: float, C: float, h_deg: float) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
    h = math.radians(h_deg)
    return (L, C * math.cos(h), C * math.sin(h))


def oklab_to_linear_srgb(L: float, a: float, b: float) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
    # Bjorn Ottosson's matrices: oklab -> LMS' (cube-root applied) -> LMS -> linear sRGB
    l_ = L + 0.3963377774 * a + 0.2158037573 * b
    m_ = L - 0.1055613458 * a - 0.0638541728 * b
    s_ = L - 0.0894841775 * a - 1.2914855480 * b

    l = l_ ** 3
    m = m_ ** 3
    s = s_ ** 3

    r = +4.0767416621 * l - 3.3077115913 * m + 0.2309699292 * s
    g = -1.2684380046 * l + 2.6097574011 * m - 0.3413193965 * s
    b_ = -0.0041960863 * l - 0.7034186147 * m + 1.7076147010 * s
    return (r, g, b_)


def linear_to_srgb(c: float) -> float:
    # IEC 61966-2-1 piecewise; clamp first.
    c = max(0.0, min(1.0, c))
    if c <= 0.0031308:
        return 12.92 * c
    return 1.055 * (c ** (1 / 2.4)) - 0.055


def srgb_to_linear(c: float) -> float:
    c = max(0.0, min(1.0, c))
    if c <= 0.04045:
        return c / 12.92
    return ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4


def oklch_to_srgb(L: float, C: float, h: float) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
    lab = oklch_to_oklab(L, C, h)
    rgb_lin = oklab_to_linear_srgb(*lab)
    return tuple(linear_to_srgb(x) for x in rgb_lin)


def relative_luminance_from_linear(r: float, g: float, b: float) -> float:
    return 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b


def relative_luminance_oklch(L: float, C: float, h: float) -> float:
    srgb = oklch_to_srgb(L, C, h)
    rgb_lin = tuple(srgb_to_linear(c) for c in srgb)
    return relative_luminance_from_linear(*rgb_lin)


def color_mix_srgb(
    a_oklch: Tuple[float, float, float],
    a_pct: float,
    b_oklch: Tuple[float, float, float],
) -> float:
    """color-mix(in srgb, A p%, B q%) -> relative luminance of the mix.

    a_pct is fractional (e.g. 0.05 for 5%); b weight is (1 - a_pct).
    """
    a_srgb = oklch_to_srgb(*a_oklch)
    b_srgb = oklch_to_srgb(*b_oklch)
    mixed_srgb = tuple(a_pct * a_srgb[i] + (1 - a_pct) * b_srgb[i] for i in range(3))
    rgb_lin = tuple(srgb_to_linear(c) for c in mixed_srgb)
    return relative_luminance_from_linear(*rgb_lin)


def contrast(l1: float, l2: float) -> float:
    hi = max(l1, l2)
    lo = min(l1, l2)
    return (hi + 0.05) / (lo + 0.05)


# ----- Pair declarations -----
# Each pair: (id, label, foreground spec, background spec)
# Foreground/background spec is a string like "ink" or a tuple
# ("mix", "accent", 0.05, "surface").
#
# M5 introduces ZERO new tokens AND ZERO new color recipes. Every pair
# below either:
#   (a) reuses an M4 measurement byte-equivalent (same tokens, same recipe);
#   (b) measures an M5-specific text-on-surface combination (e.g. boundary
#       label on the fully-bare surface, or ink-muted on the user-tinted
#       background mix used by user_turn panels).
#
# Pairs S01..S29 are the M5 surfaces. Where a pair is byte-equivalent to
# an M4 pair, the inline comment names the M4 ID for cross-reference.

PAIRS = [
    # ---------- user_turn block ----------
    ("S01", "user_turn body text",                       "ink",       ("mix", "accent", 0.05, "surface")),  # = M4 T01
    ("S02", "user_turn code-fence body",                 "ink",       "surface-raised"),                    # = M4 T19/T20
    ("S03", "user_turn inline <code>",                   "ink",       "surface-raised"),                    # = M4 T19
    ("S04", "Agent reaction <summary> chrome",           "ink-muted", "surface"),                           # = M4 T05/T22
    ("S05", "Disabled placeholder prose",                "ink-muted", "surface"),                           # = M4 T22
    ("S06", "Disabled placeholder 4px left border (NT)", "border",    "surface"),                           # SC 1.4.11 NT (decorative aid)
    ("S07", "'Expand to raw messages' summary text",     "accent",    "surface"),                           # = M4 T07/T28
    ("S08", "'Expand to raw messages' hover underline",  "accent",    "surface"),                           # text-decoration only
    # ---------- boundary block (signature detail #1) ----------
    ("S09", "Boundary label (Fraunces italic small-caps)", "ink-muted", "surface"),                         # = M4 T09
    ("S10", "Boundary 1px hairline rule (NT, SC 1.4.11)",  "border-strong", "surface"),                     # = M4 T10
    # ---------- agent_only block ----------
    ("S11", "Agent-only summary text",                   "ink-muted", "surface"),                           # = M4 T22
    ("S12", "Agent-only panel hairline border (NT)",     "border",    "surface"),                           # SC 1.4.11 NT decorative
    # ---------- oversized_user_message block ----------
    ("S13", "Oversized header text",                     "ink",       "surface"),                           # = M4 T02
    ("S14", "Oversized warn left border (NT, SC 1.4.11)", "warn",     "surface"),                           # = M4 T12
    ("S15", "Oversized verbatim <pre> body",             "ink",       "surface-raised"),                    # = M4 T06
    # ---------- truncation banner (byte-equivalent to M4) ----------
    ("S16", "Truncation banner copy",                    "ink",       ("mix", "warn", 0.08, "surface")),    # = M4 T11
    ("S17", "Truncation stripe (NT, SC 1.4.11)",         "warn",      "surface"),                           # = M4 T12
    ("S18", "'Open raw' <strong> in banner",             "ink",       ("mix", "warn", 0.08, "surface")),    # = M4 T13
    # ---------- parse-warnings banner (byte-equivalent to M4) ----------
    ("S19", "Parse-warnings <summary>",                  "ink",       "surface-raised"),                    # = M4 T14
    ("S20", "Parse-warnings <li> (mono)",                "ink-muted", "surface-raised"),                    # = M4 T15
    ("S21", "Dismiss button text",                       "ink",       "surface-raised"),                    # = M4 T16
    ("S22", "Dismiss button focus outline (NT)",         "accent",    "surface-raised"),                    # = M4 T17c
    # ---------- state-branch prose (byte-equivalent to M4) ----------
    ("S23", "'Reading session...' loading prose",        "ink-muted", "surface"),                           # = M4 T22
    ("S24", "Error prose",                               "error",     "surface"),                           # = M4 T23
    ("S25", "Retry button text",                         "ink",       "surface"),                           # = M4 T24
    ("S26", "Retry button border (NT)",                  "border-strong", "surface"),                       # = M4 T25
    ("S27", "Empty-stream prose",                        "ink-muted", "surface"),                           # = M4 T26
    ("S28", "no_raw / idle prose",                       "ink-muted", "surface"),                           # = M4 T27
    # ---------- user_turn attribution shadow (defensive)/edge ----------
    ("S29", "user_turn ink-muted on accent-tinted mix",  "ink-muted", ("mix", "accent", 0.05, "surface")),  # = M4 T03 (defensive — used only if disclosed copy lands inside the tinted panel; UNUSED in M5 baseline)
]


def luminance_for(spec, mode: str) -> float:
    pal = TOKENS[mode]
    if isinstance(spec, str):
        return relative_luminance_oklch(*pal[spec])
    elif isinstance(spec, tuple) and spec[0] == "mix":
        _, a_name, a_pct, b_name = spec
        return color_mix_srgb(pal[a_name], a_pct, pal[b_name])
    raise ValueError(f"unknown spec: {spec}")


def main():
    print(f"{'#':<5} {'Surface':<48} {'FG':<14} {'BG':<32} {'Light':>9} {'Dark':>9}")
    print("-" * 122)
    for pair_id, label, fg, bg in PAIRS:
        ratios = []
        for mode in ("light", "dark"):
            l_fg = luminance_for(fg, mode)
            l_bg = luminance_for(bg, mode)
            ratios.append(contrast(l_fg, l_bg))
        bg_repr = bg if isinstance(bg, str) else f"mix({bg[1]} {int(bg[2]*100)}%, {bg[3]})"
        fg_repr = fg if isinstance(fg, str) else str(fg)
        print(f"{pair_id:<5} {label:<48} {fg_repr:<14} {bg_repr:<32} {ratios[0]:>7.2f}:1 {ratios[1]:>7.2f}:1")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
